package com.remisoft.comm.file.annotation;

import java.lang.annotation.Documented;
import java.lang.annotation.ElementType;
import java.lang.annotation.Retention;

import java.lang.annotation.Target;

/**
 * 鍚敤鐟炵背鏂囦欢瀛樺偍妯″潡娉ㄨВ
 * <p>
 * 鍦?Spring Boot 搴旂敤涓荤被涓婃坊鍔犳娉ㄨВ锛屽惎鐢ㄧ粺涓€鏂囦欢瀛樺偍鑳藉姏锛?
 * <ul>
 *   <li>澶氬瓨鍌ㄥ悗绔細鏈湴纾佺洏 / 闃块噷浜?OSS / MinIO / 鍗庝负浜?OBS / 鑵捐浜?COS / 涓冪墰浜?/ S3 鍏煎</li>
 *   <li>鍒嗙墖涓婁紶/鏂偣缁紶锛氬熀浜庡垎鐗囷紙榛樿 5MB锛夌殑骞跺彂涓婁紶涓庢柇鐐规仮澶?/li>
 *   <li>绉掍紶锛氶€氳繃鏂囦欢鎸囩汗锛圫HA-256锛夊幓閲?/li>
 *   <li>鐩綍鏍戯細鏀寔閫掑綊鏌ヨ鐩綍缁撴瀯</li>
 *   <li>鎵归噺涓嬭浇/鍒犻櫎锛氬師瀛愭€ф搷浣?/li>
 *   <li>鏂囦欢鎸囩汗鍘婚噸鏈嶅姟锛氳法鐢ㄦ埛銆佽法瀛樺偍鐨勯噸澶嶆枃浠舵竻鐞?/li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b></p>
 * <pre>{@code
 * @SpringBootApplication
 * @EnableRemiFile
 * public class Application {
 *     public static void main(String[] args) {
 *         SpringApplication.run(Application.class, args);
 *     }
 * }
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 * @see com.remisoft.comm.file.config.FileConfiguration
 */
@Target(ElementType.TYPE)
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Documented
@Import(FileConfiguration.class)
public @interface EnableRemiFile {
}

package com.remisoft.comm.file.callback;

/**
 * 鏂囦欢涓婁紶杩涘害鍥炶皟鎺ュ彛
 * <p>鐩戝惉涓婁紶杩囩▼涓殑鍏抽敭鑺傜偣锛屼娇涓氬姟灞傝兘澶熻幏鍙栧疄鏃惰繘搴︼紙鐢ㄤ簬杩涘害鏉°€佸急缃戦噸璇曠瓑鍦烘櫙锛夈€?
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 */
public interface UploadProgressListener {

    /**
     * 涓婁紶寮€濮嬪墠鍥炶皟
     *
     * @param totalBytes 鏂囦欢鎬诲瓧鑺傛暟
     */
    void onStart(long totalBytes);

    /**
     * 涓婁紶杩涜涓洖璋冿紙姣忚鍙栦竴涓紦鍐插尯瑙﹀彂涓€娆★級
     *
     * @param uploadedBytes 宸插啓鍏ュ瓧鑺傛暟
     * @param totalBytes    鏂囦欢鎬诲瓧鑺傛暟
     */
    void onProgress(long uploadedBytes, long totalBytes);

    /**
     * 涓婁紶鎴愬姛鍥炶皟
     *
     * @param objectName 瀛樺偍閿?
     */
    void onSuccess(String objectName);

    /**
     * 涓婁紶澶辫触鍥炶皟
     *
     * @param objectName 瀛樺偍閿?
     * @param cause      寮傚父鍘熷洜
     */
    void onFailure(String objectName, Throwable cause);
}

package com.remisoft.comm.file.config;

import com.remisoft.comm.file.health.FileHealthIndicator;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.service.DedupCleanupScheduler;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.service.FileDedupService;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.CheckpointService;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.CheckpointStore;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.DelegatingCheckpointStore;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.DelegatingMultipartContextStore;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.DefaultCheckpointService;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.IFileStorageProvider;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.IStorageFactory;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.LocalCheckpointStore;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.MultipartContextStore;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.RedisCheckpointStore;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.RedisMultipartContextStore;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.UploadConcurrencyGuard;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.util.FileTypeValidator;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.ObjectProvider;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.AutoConfiguration;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnBean;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnClass;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnMissingBean;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnProperty;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.EnableConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;

/**
 * 鏂囦欢瀛樺偍鑷姩閰嶇疆绫? *
 * <p>鍩轰簬 Spring Boot AutoConfiguration 鏈哄埗锛岃嚜鍔ㄨ閰嶆枃浠跺瓨鍌ㄧ浉鍏崇殑 Bean锛? * 鍖呮嫭瀛樺偍宸ュ巶銆佸垎鐗囦笂浼犱笂涓嬫枃瀛樺偍銆佹鏌ョ偣瀛樺偍銆佸仴搴锋鏌ョ瓑缁勪欢銆? *
 * <p><b>涓昏鐗规€э細</b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>鏅鸿兘闄嶇骇锛歊edis 鍙敤鏃朵紭鍏堜娇鐢?Redis 瀛樺偍锛屽惁鍒欓檷绾у埌鍐呭瓨鎴栨湰鍦版枃浠?/li>
 *   <li>骞跺彂鎺у埗锛氬彲閫夌殑涓婁紶骞跺彂淇濇姢鍣紝闃叉鍚屼竴鏂囦欢琚噸澶嶄笂浼?/li>
 *   <li>鍋ュ悍妫€鏌ワ細闆嗘垚 Spring Boot Actuator锛岃嚜鍔ㄦ毚闇叉枃浠跺瓨鍌ㄥ仴搴风姸鎬?/li>
 *   <li>瀹氭椂娓呯悊锛氭瘡灏忔椂鑷姩娓呯悊杩囨湡鐨勫垎鐗囦笂浼犱笂涓嬫枃</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 */
@AutoConfiguration
@EnableConfigurationProperties({FileProperties.class, FileUploadProperties.class})
@ConditionalOnProperty(prefix = "remi.file", name = "enabled", havingValue = "true", matchIfMissing = true)
public class FileConfiguration {

    /** 鍒嗙墖涓婁紶涓婁笅鏂囪繃鏈熸椂闂达紙鍒嗛挓锛?*/
    private static final int MULTIPART_CONTEXT_TIMEOUT_MINUTES = 60;

    /** 鍒嗙墖涓婁紶涓婁笅鏂囧瓨鍌紙鐢?Spring 鑷姩娉ㄥ叆锛?*/
    private MultipartContextStore multipartContextStore;

    private static final Logger configLog = LoggerFactory.getLogger(FileConfiguration.class);

    /**
     * 鍒嗙墖涓婁紶涓婁笅鏂囧瓨鍌?Bean
     * <p>褰?Redis 鍙敤鏃朵紭鍏堜娇鐢?Redis 瀛樺偍锛屽惁鍒欓檷绾у埌鍐呭瓨 Map銆?     */
    @Bean
    @ConditionalOnMissingBean(MultipartContextStore.class)
    public MultipartContextStore multipartContextStore(
            ObjectProvider<StringRedisTemplate> stringRedisTemplateProvider) {
        StringRedisTemplate template = stringRedisTemplateProvider.getIfAvailable();
        if (template != null) {
            return new DelegatingMultipartContextStore(
                    new RedisMultipartContextStore(template), null);
        }
        configLog.warn("[FileConfig] Redis not available, falling back to InMemoryMultipartContextStore. " +
                "Multi-instance deployments may fail to share multipart upload contexts.");
        return new com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.InMemoryMultipartContextStore();
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ョ偣瀛樺偍 Bean
     * <p>褰?Redis 鍙敤鏃朵紭鍏堜娇鐢?Redis 瀛樺偍锛屽惁鍒欓檷绾у埌鏈湴鏂囦欢銆?     */
    @Bean
    @ConditionalOnMissingBean(CheckpointStore.class)
    public CheckpointStore checkpointStore(
            FileProperties fileProperties,
            ObjectProvider<StringRedisTemplate> stringRedisTemplateProvider) {
        StringRedisTemplate template = stringRedisTemplateProvider.getIfAvailable();
        CheckpointStore fallback = new LocalCheckpointStore(fileProperties.getCheckpointDir());
        if (template != null) {
            return new DelegatingCheckpointStore(
                    new RedisCheckpointStore(template), fallback);
        }
        configLog.warn("[FileConfig] Redis not available, falling back to LocalCheckpointStore. " +
                "Multi-instance deployments may fail to share checkpoint data for resumable uploads.");
        return fallback;
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ョ偣鏈嶅姟 Bean
     * <p>鍩轰簬 CheckpointStore 鏋勫缓鏈嶅姟灞傚皝瑁呫€?     */
    @Bean
    @ConditionalOnMissingBean(CheckpointService.class)
    public CheckpointService checkpointService(
            CheckpointStore store,
            MultipartContextStore multipartStore) {
        final long checkpointTtl = 24 * 3600L;
        return new DefaultCheckpointService(store,
                (bucketName, objectName, uploadId) -> {
                    // 鍦?Bean 鍒濆鍖栭樁娈垫棤娉曡幏鍙?IFileStorageProvider锛岃繑鍥炵┖鍒楄〃
                    // 瀹為檯杩愯鏃剁敱 AbstractFileStorage 閲嶆柊鍒涘缓鏃朵娇鐢ㄧ湡瀹?listParts
                    return java.util.Collections.emptyList();
                },
                checkpointTtl);
    }

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓鏂囦欢瀛樺偍鎻愪緵鑰呭伐鍘?     * <p>鏍规嵁閰嶇疆鍒濆鍖?Magic Number 鏍￠獙寮€鍏筹紝鍒涘缓瀛樺偍宸ュ巶骞舵敞鍏ュ垎甯冨紡瀛樺偍瀹炵幇鍜屽苟鍙戜繚鎶ゅ櫒銆?     *
     * @param fileProperties              鏂囦欢瀛樺偍鍩虹閰嶇疆
     * @param fileUploadProperties        鏂囦欢涓婁紶閰嶇疆
     * @param multipartContextStore       鍒嗙墖涓婁紶涓婁笅鏂囧瓨鍌?     * @param checkpointStore             妫€鏌ョ偣瀛樺偍
     * @param checkpointService           妫€鏌ョ偣鏈嶅姟
     * @param stringRedisTemplateProvider Redis 妯℃澘鎻愪緵鑰咃紙鍙€夛級
     * @return 鏂囦欢瀛樺偍鎻愪緵鑰呭疄渚?     */
    @Bean
    @ConditionalOnMissingBean(IFileStorageProvider.class)
    public IFileStorageProvider fileStorageProvider(FileProperties fileProperties,
                                                     FileUploadProperties fileUploadProperties,
                                                     MultipartContextStore multipartContextStore,
                                                     CheckpointStore checkpointStore,
                                                     CheckpointService checkpointService,
                                                     ObjectProvider<StringRedisTemplate> stringRedisTemplateProvider) {
        // 浠庨厤缃枃浠跺垵濮嬪寲 Magic Number 鏍￠獙寮€鍏?        FileTypeValidator.init(fileProperties.isCheckMagicNumber());

        IStorageFactory factory = new IStorageFactory(fileProperties, fileUploadProperties);
        // 閫氱煡鎵€鏈夊凡娉ㄥ唽鐨勫瓨鍌ㄥ疄渚嬩娇鐢ㄥ垎甯冨紡瀛樺偍
        factory.setMultipartContextStore(multipartContextStore);
        factory.setCheckpointStore(checkpointStore);
        // 娉ㄥ叆鏈嶅姟灞?        factory.setCheckpointService(checkpointService);

        // 娉ㄥ叆骞跺彂涓婁紶淇濇姢鍣紙浠呭綋 Redis 鍙敤涓旈厤缃惎鐢ㄦ椂锛?        UploadConcurrencyGuard guard = buildConcurrencyGuardIfEnabled(fileProperties, stringRedisTemplateProvider.getIfAvailable());
        if (guard != null) {
            factory.setConcurrencyGuard(guard);
        }

        return factory;
    }

    /**
     * 鏋勫缓骞跺彂涓婁紶淇濇姢鍣紙Redis 涓嶅彲鐢ㄦ垨閰嶇疆鏈惎鐢ㄦ椂杩斿洖 null锛?     */
    private UploadConcurrencyGuard buildConcurrencyGuardIfEnabled(FileProperties fileProperties, StringRedisTemplate redisTemplate) {
        if (redisTemplate == null) {
            return null;
        }
        var config = fileProperties.getConcurrencyControl();
        if (config == null || !config.isEnabled()) {
            return null;
        }
        return new UploadConcurrencyGuard(redisTemplate, config);
    }

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓鏂囦欢瀛樺偍鍋ュ悍妫€鏌ユ寚绀哄櫒
     *
     * @param fileStorageProvider 鏂囦欢瀛樺偍鎻愪緵鑰?     * @param fileProperties      鏂囦欢瀛樺偍閰嶇疆
     * @return 鏂囦欢鍋ュ悍妫€鏌ユ寚绀哄櫒瀹炰緥
     */
    @Bean
    @ConditionalOnClass(name = "org.springframework.boot.actuate.health.HealthIndicator")
    @ConditionalOnMissingBean(FileHealthIndicator.class)
    public FileHealthIndicator storageHealthIndicator(IFileStorageProvider fileStorageProvider,
                                                      FileProperties fileProperties) {
        return new FileHealthIndicator(fileStorageProvider, fileProperties);
    }

    /**
     * 鍘婚噸娓呯悊璋冨害鍣?Bean
     * <p>浠呭綋 FileDedupService 瀛樺湪鏃舵敞鍐岋紝瀹氭椂娓呯悊杩囨湡鐨勫幓閲嶆槧灏勮褰曘€?     */
    @Bean
    @ConditionalOnBean(FileDedupService.class)
    public DedupCleanupScheduler dedupCleanupScheduler(FileDedupService fileDedupService) {
        return new DedupCleanupScheduler(fileDedupService);
    }

    /**
     * 瀹氭椂娓呯悊杩囨湡鐨勫垎鐗囦笂浼犱笂涓嬫枃锛堟瘡灏忔椂鎵ц涓€娆★級
     */
    @Scheduled(fixedRate = 3_600_000)
    public void cleanExpiredMultipartContexts() {
        if (multipartContextStore == null) return;
        multipartContextStore.cleanExpired(MULTIPART_CONTEXT_TIMEOUT_MINUTES);
    }
}

package com.remisoft.comm.file.config;

import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.StorageType;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Max;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Min;
import lombok.Data;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.validation.annotation.Validated;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 鏂囦欢瀛樺偍閰嶇疆灞炴€х被
 * <p>
 * 瀹氫箟鏂囦欢瀛樺偍鐨勫悇绉嶉厤缃弬鏁帮紝鏀寔澶氱瀛樺偍骞冲彴锛氭湰鍦板瓨鍌ㄣ€丮inIO銆侀樋閲?OSS銆? * 鑵捐 COS銆佷竷鐗涗簯銆丼3 鍏煎瀛樺偍銆佸崕涓?OBS銆? * </p>
 *
 * <p><b>閰嶇疆绀轰緥锛坅pplication.yml锛夛細</b></p>
 * <pre>{@code
 * remi:
 *   file:
 *     type: minio
 *     endpoint: http://localhost:9000
 *     access-key: minioadmin
 *     secret-key: minioadmin
 *     bucket: remi-files
 *     domain: https://files.example.com
 *     allowed-suffixes: png,jpg,pdf,docx
 *     max-file-size: 104857600
 *     part-size: 5242880
 * }</pre>
 *
 * <p><b>鍒嗙墖澶у皬锛?/b>榛樿 5MB锛堜笌 S3 鍗忚瀵归綈锛夈€傚垎鐗囪繃灏忎細鏄捐憲澧炲姞璇锋眰鏁帮紝
 * 杩囧ぇ鍒欓檷浣庡苟鍙戝害涓庡け璐ユ仮澶嶆晥鐜囥€?/p>
 *
 * <p><b>瀹夊叏绾︽潫锛?/b>{@code accessKey} / {@code secretKey} 寮虹儓寤鸿閫氳繃
 * 鐜鍙橀噺鎴?Vault 娉ㄥ叆锛屼笉瑕佺‖缂栫爜鍦ㄩ厤缃枃浠朵腑銆?/p>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 * @see StorageType
 */
@Data
@Validated
@ConfigurationProperties(prefix = "remi.file")
public class FileProperties {

    /**
     * 鏄惁鍚敤鏂囦欢瀛樺偍妯″潡锛堥粯璁?true锛?     *
     * <p>鍏抽棴鍚庯紝{@code FileConfiguration} 鍙婄浉鍏?Bean 涓嶄細鑷姩娉ㄥ唽锛?     * {@code FileStorageHealthIndicator} 涔熼渶瑕佹樉寮忚缃负 true 鎵嶄細鍚敤銆?     */
    private boolean enabled = true;

    /**
     * 瀛樺偍绫诲瀷
     * <p>鍙€夊€硷細local锛堟湰鍦帮級銆乵inio銆乷ss / aliyun锛堥樋閲屼簯锛夈€乧os / tencent-cos锛堣吘璁簯锛夈€?     * qiniu锛堜竷鐗涗簯锛夈€乻3 / aws-s3锛圓WS S3 鍏煎锛夈€乷bs / huawei-obs锛堝崕涓轰簯锛?     */
    private String type = StorageType.MINIO;

    /** 鏂囦欢鏈嶅姟鍣ㄥ湴鍧€锛堜簯瀛樺偍鐨?endpoint锛屽 http://localhost:9000锛?*/
    private String endpoint;

    /** 璁块棶瀵嗛挜 AccessKey锛堢敤浜庤韩浠借璇侊紱寤鸿浠庣幆澧冨彉閲忔敞鍏ワ級 */
    private String accessKey;

    /** 绉樺瘑瀵嗛挜 SecretKey锛堢敤浜庤韩浠借璇侊紱寤鸿浠庣幆澧冨彉閲忔敞鍏ワ級 */
    private String secretKey;

    /** 瀛樺偍妗跺悕绉帮紙鐢ㄤ簬闅旂涓嶅悓涓氬姟鐨勬枃浠讹級 */
    private String bucket;

    /** 璁块棶鍩熷悕锛堟枃浠惰闂殑鍩熷悕鎴?Nginx 浠ｇ悊鍦板潃锛?*/
    private String domain;

    /** 鍖哄煙淇℃伅锛堜簯瀛樺偍鐨勫尯鍩熼厤缃紝閮ㄥ垎浜戝瓨鍌ㄩ渶瑕佹寚瀹氬尯鍩燂級 */
    private String region;

    /** 鍏佽涓婁紶鐨勬枃浠跺悗缂€鍒楄〃锛堜负绌烘椂浣跨敤榛樿鐧藉悕鍗曪紱閰嶇疆鍚庝互褰撳墠鍒楄〃涓哄噯锛?*/
    private List<String> allowedSuffixes = new ArrayList<>();

    /** 鍗曟枃浠舵渶澶уぇ灏忥紙瀛楄妭锛岄粯璁?100MB = 104857600 瀛楄妭锛?*/
    private Long maxFileSize = 104857600L;

    /** 鏈€澶ц姹傚ぇ灏忥紙瀛楄妭锛岄粯璁?100MB锛?*/
    private long maxRequestSize = 100 * 1024 * 1024;

    /** 杩炴帴瓒呮椂鏃堕棿锛堟绉掞紝榛樿 30s锛?*/
    private Integer connectionTimeout = 30000;

=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\file\domain\BatchDeleteResult.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.file.domain;

import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * 鎵归噺鍒犻櫎缁撴灉
 *
 * <p>鍖呭惈鎴愬姛鍒犻櫎鐨勫璞¤矾寰勫垪琛ㄥ拰澶辫触鐨勫璞¤矾寰勫垪琛紙鍚け璐ュ師鍥狅級銆?
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 */
public record BatchDeleteResult(
        List<String> successList,
        Map<String, String> failedList
) {

    /**
     * 鍏ㄩ儴鎴愬姛鐨勪究鎹锋瀯閫犳柟娉?
     */
    public static BatchDeleteResult allSuccess(List<String> deletedPaths) {
        return new BatchDeleteResult(
                List.copyOf(deletedPaths),
                Collections.emptyMap()
        );
    }

    /**
     * 鍏ㄩ儴澶辫触鐨勪究鎹锋瀯閫犳柟娉?
     */
    public static BatchDeleteResult allFailed(Map<String, String> errors) {
        return new BatchDeleteResult(
                Collections.emptyList(),
                errors
        );
    }

    /**
     * 鏄惁鏈夊け璐ラ」
     */
    public boolean hasFailures() {
        return !failedList.isEmpty();
    }

    /**
     * 鎴愬姛鍒犻櫎鐨勬暟閲?
     */
    public int successCount() {
        return successList.size();
    }

    /**
     * 澶辫触鍒犻櫎鐨勬暟閲?
     */
    public int failureCount() {
        return failedList.size();
    }

    /**
     * 鏄惁鍏ㄩ儴鎴愬姛
     */
    public boolean allSuccess() {
        return failedList.isEmpty();
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇澶辫触鎽樿淇℃伅
     */
    public String getFailureSummary() {
        if (failedList.isEmpty()) {
            return "鍏ㄩ儴鎴愬姛";
        }
        return failedList.entrySet().stream()
                .map(e -> e.getKey() + ": " + e.getValue())
                .collect(Collectors.joining("; "));
    }
}

=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\file\domain\FileStorage.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.file.domain;

import com.remisoft.comm.exception.custom.BusinessException;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.constant.FileConstant;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.exception.FileExceptionCode;
import com.remisoft.comm.util.file.FileTypeUtils;
import lombok.Data;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MultipartFile;

import java.io.Serializable;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

/**
 * 鏂囦欢瀛樺偍绠＄悊瀹炰綋绫?
 * <p>
 * 瀛樺偍鏂囦欢鐨勫熀鏈俊鎭細鏂囦欢鏍囪瘑銆佸悕绉般€佸ぇ灏忋€佺被鍨嬨€佷笂浼犳椂闂淬€佸瓨鍌ㄤ綅缃瓑銆?
 * 涓氬姟灞傚熀浜庢瀹炰綋瀹炵幇鏂囦欢鍏冧俊鎭鐞嗭紙鏁版嵁搴?/ Redis 缂撳瓨锛夈€?
 * </p>
 *
 * <p><b>鏍稿績瀛楁锛?/b></p>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>{@link #id} - 鏂囦欢鍞竴鏍囪瘑锛堥洩鑺辩畻娉曠敓鎴愶級</li>
 *   <li>{@link #parentId} - 鐖剁洰褰曟爣璇?/li>
 *   <li>{@link #url} - 鏂囦欢璁块棶鍦板潃</li>
 *   <li>{@link #fileName} - 鍘熷鏂囦欢鍚嶏紙鍚師濮嬪悗缂€锛?/li>
 *   <li>{@link #uuidName} - 瀛樺偍绔敓鎴愮殑鍞竴瀵硅薄鍚嶏紙闃查噸鍚?+ 闃茶矾寰勯亶鍘嗭級</li>
 *   <li>{@link #fileRename} - 閲嶅懡鍚嶅悗鐨勬枃浠跺悕</li>
 *   <li>{@link #suffix} - 鏂囦欢鎵╁睍鍚嶏紙灏忓啓锛?/li>
 *   <li>{@link #dirIds} - 鎵€灞炵洰褰曡矾寰勬爣璇?/li>
 *   <li>{@link #isDir} - 鏄惁涓虹洰褰曪紙1=鏄紝0=鍚︼級</li>
 *   <li>{@link #isImage} / {@link #isVideo} / {@link #isAudio} / {@link #isOffice} / {@link #isCode} - 鏂囦欢绫诲瀷鏍囪</li>
 *   <li>{@link #size} - 鏂囦欢澶у皬锛堝瓧鑺傦級</li>
 *   <li>{@link #type} - 鏂囦欢鍒嗙被锛堝 image銆乿ideo銆乧ode 绛夛級</li>
 *   <li>{@link #uploadAt} - 涓婁紶鏃堕棿</li>
 *   <li>{@link #source} - 鏂囦欢鏉ユ簮锛堢郴缁熷悕 / 涓氬姟妯″潡锛?/li>
 *   <li>{@link #mimeType} - MIME Type锛堝 image/png銆乤pplication/pdf锛?/li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>瀹夊叏绾︽潫锛?/b>{@link #uuidName} 搴斿熀浜?UUID/闆姳 ID 閲嶆柊鐢熸垚锛?
 * 涓嶄娇鐢ㄧ敤鎴疯緭鍏ョ殑鍘熷鏂囦欢鍚嶄綔涓哄瓨鍌?Key锛岄伩鍏嶈矾寰勯亶鍘嗘敾鍑汇€?/p>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Data
public class FileStorage implements Serializable {

    private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;

    /** 鏂囦欢鍞竴鏍囪瘑 */
    private String id;

    /** 鐖剁洰褰曟爣璇嗭紙鐢ㄤ簬鏋勫缓鐩綍鏍戯級 */
    private String parentId;

    /** 鏂囦欢璁块棶鍦板潃 */
    private String url;

    /** 鍘熷鏂囦欢鍚嶏紙鐢ㄦ埛涓婁紶鏃剁殑鍘熷鏂囦欢鍚嶏級 */
    private String fileName;

    /** 瀛樺偍绔敓鎴愮殑鍞竴瀵硅薄鍚嶏紙鍩轰簬 UUID/闆姳 ID 閲嶆柊鐢熸垚锛岄槻璺緞閬嶅巻锛?*/
    private String uuidName;

    /** 閲嶅懡鍚嶅悗鐨勬枃浠跺悕锛堜笟鍔″彲瀹氬埗鐨勫睍绀哄悕锛?*/
    private String fileRename;

    /** 鏂囦欢鎵╁睍鍚嶏紙缁熶竴灏忓啓锛?*/
    private String suffix;

    /** 鎵€灞炵洰褰曡矾寰勬爣璇嗭紙澶氱骇鐩綍鏃朵娇鐢級 */
    private String dirIds;

    /** 鏄惁涓虹洰褰曪紙1=鏄紝0=鍚︼級 */
    private Integer isDir;

    /** 鏄惁涓哄浘鐗囷紙1=鏄紝0=鍚︼級 */
    private Integer isImage;

    /** 鏄惁涓鸿棰戯紙1=鏄紝0=鍚︼級 */
    private Integer isVideo;

    /** 鏄惁涓洪煶棰戯紙1=鏄紝0=鍚︼級 */
    private Integer isAudio;

    /** 鏄惁涓哄姙鍏枃妗ｏ紙1=鏄紝0=鍚︼級 */
    private Integer isOffice;

    /** 鏄惁涓轰唬鐮佹枃浠讹紙1=鏄紝0=鍚︼級 */
    private Integer isCode;

    /** 鏂囦欢澶у皬锛堝瓧鑺傦級 */
    private Long size;

    /** 鏂囦欢鍒嗙被锛堝 image銆乿ideo銆乧ode 绛夛級 */
    private String type;

    /** MIME Type锛堝 image/png銆乤pplication/pdf锛?*/
    private String mimeType;

    /** 涓婁紶鏃堕棿 */
    private LocalDateTime uploadAt;

    /** 鏂囦欢鏉ユ簮锛堢郴缁熷悕 / 涓氬姟妯″潡锛?*/
    private String source;

    /**
     * 浠?MultipartFile 鏋勫缓 FileStorage 瀹炰綋
     * <p>鑷姩鎻愬彇鏂囦欢鍚嶃€佹墿灞曞悕銆佸ぇ灏忋€佺被鍨嬬瓑淇℃伅锛屽苟鏍￠獙鏂囦欢鍚堟硶鎬у拰鎵╁睍鍚嶇櫧鍚嶅崟銆?
     *
     * @param file 涓婁紶鐨勬枃浠?
     * @return 濉厖浜嗗熀鏈俊鎭殑 FileStorage 瀹炰綋
     * @throws BusinessException 鏂囦欢涓虹┖銆佹枃浠跺悕鏃犳晥鎴栨墿灞曞悕涓嶅厑璁告椂鎶涘嚭
     */
    public static FileStorage build(MultipartFile file) {
        if (file.isEmpty()) {
            throw new BusinessException(FileExceptionCode.FILE_EMPTY);
        }
        String originalFilename = file.getOriginalFilename();
        if (originalFilename == null || originalFilename.isEmpty()) {
            throw new BusinessException(FileExceptionCode.FILE_NAME_INVALID);
        }
        int dotPos = originalFilename.lastIndexOf(FileConstant.SUFFIX_SPLIT);
        if (dotPos < 0) {
            throw new BusinessException(FileExceptionCode.FILE_NAME_INVALID);
        }
        Long size = file.getSize();
        String orgName = file.getOriginalFilename();
        String fileExt = FileTypeUtils.getFileType(orgName);
        if (!FileTypeUtils.isAllowedExtension(fileExt)) {
            throw new BusinessException(FileExceptionCode.FILE_SUFFIX_NOT_ALLOWED);
        }
        String type;
        if (FileTypeUtils.isCodeExtension(fileExt)) {
            type = "code";
        } else {
            type = fileExt;
        }
        FileStorage fileStorage = new FileStorage();
        fileStorage.setSuffix(fileExt);
        fileStorage.setSize(size);
        fileStorage.setFileName(orgName);
        fileStorage.setIsImage(FileTypeUtils.isImageExtension(fileExt) ? 1 : 0);
        fileStorage.setIsVideo(FileTypeUtils.isVideoExtension(fileExt) ? 1 : 0);
        fileStorage.setIsAudio(FileTypeUtils.isAudioExtension(fileExt) ? 1 : 0);
        fileStorage.setIsOffice(FileTypeUtils.isOfficeExtension(fileExt) ? 1 : 0);
        fileStorage.setIsDir(0);
        fileStorage.setUploadAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        fileStorage.setType(type);
        return fileStorage;
    }
}

import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;

import lombok.NoArgsConstructor;

import java.util.List;

/**

package com.remisoft.comm.file.domain;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonIgnore;
import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Data;
import lombok.NoArgsConstructor;

import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 鍒嗙墖涓婁紶妫€鏌ョ偣淇℃伅
 * <p>璁板綍鍒嗙墖涓婁紶鐨勮繘搴︼紝鏀寔鏂偣缁紶銆?
 * 褰撲笂浼犱腑鏂悗锛屽彲閫氳繃璇诲彇妫€鏌ョ偣淇℃伅鎭㈠涓婁紶杩涘害銆?
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 */
@Data
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
public class UploadCheckpoint {

    /**
     * 涓婁紶浠诲姟鍞竴鏍囪瘑
     */
    private String taskId;

    /**
     * 瀛樺偍妗跺悕绉?
     */
    private String bucketName;

    /**
     * 瀵硅薄璺緞
     */
    private String objectName;

    /**
     * 鍒嗙墖涓婁紶ID锛堜簯瀛樺偍杩斿洖锛?
     */
    private String uploadId;

    /**
     * 鏂囦欢鎬诲ぇ灏忥紙瀛楄妭锛?
     */
    private Long totalSize;

    /**
     * 鏂囦欢鍘熷鍚嶇О
     */
    private String fileName;

    /**
     * 鏂囦欢鍐呭绫诲瀷
     */
    private String contentType;

    /**
     * 鍒嗙墖澶у皬锛堝瓧鑺傦級
     */
    private Long partSize;

    /**
     * 宸蹭笂浼犵殑鍒嗙墖淇℃伅
     */
    private List<UploadedPart> uploadedParts;

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓鏃堕棿
     */
    private LocalDateTime createTime;

    /**
     * 鏈€鍚庝慨鏀规椂闂?
     */
    private LocalDateTime lastModifyTime;

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ョ偣鏂囦欢璺緞
     */
    private String checkpointFilePath;

    /**
     * 宸蹭笂浼犲瓧鑺傛暟
     */
    private Long uploadedBytes;

    /**
     * 宸蹭笂浼犲垎鐗囨暟
     */
    private Integer uploadedPartsCount;

    /**
     * 鏁翠釜鏂囦欢鐨?MD5 鏍￠獙鍊硷紙鐢ㄤ簬鍚堝苟瀹屾垚鍚庢牎楠屾枃浠跺畬鏁存€э級
     */
    private String fileMd5;

    /**
     * 鍒嗙墖涓婁紶杩囩▼涓疮绉殑鏂囦欢 MD5锛堝崄鍏繘鍒跺瓧绗︿覆锛?
     * <p>姣忎笂浼犱竴涓垎鐗囧氨閫氳繃 MessageDigest.update 绱Н璁＄畻锛?
     * 鍚堝苟瀹屾垚鍚庣洿鎺ヤ娇鐢ㄨ鍊间笌 fileMd5 瀵规瘮锛屾棤闇€閲嶆柊涓嬭浇鏁翠釜鏂囦欢銆?
     * 鑻ヤ负 null 鍒欏洖閫€鍒伴噸鏂颁笅杞界殑鏃ч€昏緫锛堝吋瀹规棫鐗堟湰锛夈€?
     */
    private String accumulatedMd5Hex;

    /**
     * 璁＄畻涓婁紶杩涘害鐧惧垎姣?
     * <p>璇ユ柟娉曚负璁＄畻灞炴€э紝涓嶅弬涓庡簭鍒楀寲</p>
     *
     * @return 杩涘害鐧惧垎姣旓紙0-100锛?
     */
    @JsonIgnore
    public int getProgressPercent() {
        if (totalSize == null || totalSize == 0) {
            return 0;
        }
        return (int) ((uploadedBytes != null ? uploadedBytes : 0) * 100 / totalSize);
    }

    /**
     * 宸蹭笂浼犲垎鐗囦俊鎭?
     */
    @Data
    @NoArgsConstructor
    @AllArgsConstructor
    public static class UploadedPart {

        /**
         * 鍒嗙墖缂栧彿锛堜粠1寮€濮嬶級
         */
        private Integer partNumber;

        /**
         * 鍒嗙墖澶у皬锛堝瓧鑺傦級
         */
        private Long size;

        /**
         * 鍒嗙墖 ETag锛堜簯瀛樺偍杩斿洖锛?
         */
        private String eTag;

        /**
         * 鏈湴鍒嗙墖鏂囦欢璺緞
         */
        private String localFilePath;

        /**
         * 鏄惁宸蹭笂浼犲畬鎴?
         */
        private Boolean uploaded;

        /**
         * 鍒嗙墖鏁版嵁鐨?MD5 鏍￠獙鍊?
         */
        private String chunkMd5;
    }

    /**
     * 鏍￠獙鏁版嵁鐨?MD5 鏄惁涓庨鏈熷€间竴鑷?
     *
     * @param data         鏁版嵁瀛楄妭鏁扮粍
     * @param expectedMd5  棰勬湡鐨?MD5 鍊硷紙鍗佸叚杩涘埗瀛楃涓诧紝涓嶅尯鍒嗗ぇ灏忓啓锛?
     * @return true 琛ㄧず鏍￠獙閫氳繃锛宖alse 琛ㄧず涓嶅尮閰?
     */
    public static boolean validateMd5(byte[] data, String expectedMd5) {
        if (expectedMd5 == null || expectedMd5.isEmpty()) {
            return true;
        }
        if (data == null || data.length == 0) {
            return false;
        }
        try {
            MessageDigest md = MessageDigest.getInstance("MD5");
            byte[] digest = md.digest(data);
            String actualMd5 = bytesToHex(digest);
            return actualMd5.equalsIgnoreCase(expectedMd5);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 璁＄畻瀛楄妭鏁扮粍鐨?MD5锛堝崄鍏繘鍒跺皬鍐欏瓧绗︿覆锛?
     *
     * @param data 鏁版嵁瀛楄妭鏁扮粍
     * @return MD5 瀛楃涓?
     */
    public static String calculateMd5(byte[] data) {
        if (data == null || data.length == 0) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            MessageDigest md = MessageDigest.getInstance("MD5");
            byte[] digest = md.digest(data);
            return bytesToHex(digest);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    private static String bytesToHex(byte[] bytes) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(bytes.length * 2);
        for (byte b : bytes) {
            sb.append(String.format("%02x", b));
        }
        return sb.toString();
    }
}

package com.remisoft.comm.file.exception;

/**
 * 鏂囦欢鏍￠獙寮傚父銆?
 * <p>
 * 褰撴枃浠朵笂浼犳牎楠屽け璐ユ椂锛堝鏂囦欢澶у皬瓒呴檺銆佹墿灞曞悕涓嶅厑璁搞€丆ontent-Type 涓嶅尮閰嶇瓑锛夋姏鍑烘寮傚父銆?
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 */
public class FileValidationException extends RuntimeException {

    private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;

    /**
     * 鏋勯€犳枃浠舵牎楠屽紓甯?
     *
     * @param message 寮傚父淇℃伅
     */
    public FileValidationException(String message) {
        super(message);
    }

    /**
     * 鏋勯€犳枃浠舵牎楠屽紓甯革紙甯﹀師鍥狅級
     *
     * @param message 寮傚父淇℃伅
     * @param cause   瀵艰嚧姝ゅ紓甯哥殑鍘熷鍘熷洜
     */
    public FileValidationException(String message, Throwable cause) {
        super(message, cause);
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\file\health\FileHealthIndicator.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.file.health;

import com.remisoft.comm.file.config.FileProperties;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.IFileStorage;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.IFileStorageProvider;
import com.remisoft.comm.util.string.StringUtils;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.boot.actuate.health.Health;
import org.springframework.boot.actuate.health.HealthIndicator;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnClass;

/**
 * 瀛樺偍鍚庣鍋ュ悍妫€鏌ユ寚绀哄櫒
 * <p>娉ㄥ唽鍒?Spring Boot Actuator 鍋ュ悍绔偣锛?actuator/health锛夛紝
 * 妫€鏌ュ瓨鍌ㄨ繛鎺ュ彲鐢ㄦ€т笌 bucket 鏄惁瀛樺湪銆?
 *
 * <p>浠呭湪寮曞叆 spring-boot-actuator 渚濊禆鏃剁敓鏁堬紙閫氳繃 @ConditionalOnClass 鎺у埗锛夈€?
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 */
@Slf4j
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@ConditionalOnClass(name = "org.springframework.boot.actuate.health.HealthIndicator")
public class FileHealthIndicator implements HealthIndicator {

    private final IFileStorageProvider fileStorageProvider;
    private final FileProperties fileProperties;

    @Override
    public Health health() {
        try {
            IFileStorage storage = fileStorageProvider.getStorage();
            String bucketName = fileProperties.getBucket();
            // 鏈厤缃?bucket 鏃朵粎杩斿洖瀛樺偍绫诲瀷淇℃伅锛屼笉鎵ц鎺㈡祴
            if (StringUtils.isBlank(bucketName)) {
                return Health.up()
                        .withDetail("storageType", storage.getClass().getSimpleName())
                        .withDetail("bucketConfigured", false)
                        .build();
            }
            // 鎺㈡祴 bucket 鏄惁瀛樺湪
            boolean bucketExists = storage.bucketExists(bucketName);
            return Health.up()
                    .withDetail("storageType", storage.getClass().getSimpleName())
                    .withDetail("bucketExists", bucketExists)
                    .withDetail("bucketConfigured", true)
                    .build();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("[StorageHealthIndicator] storage health check failed: {}", e.getMessage());
            return Health.down()
                    .withDetail("error", e.getMessage())
                    .build();
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\file\health\FileStorageHealthIndicator.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.file.health;

import com.remisoft.comm.file.config.FileProperties;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.IFileStorage;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.IStorageFactory;
import jakarta.annotation.Resource;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.boot.actuate.health.Health;
import org.springframework.boot.actuate.health.HealthIndicator;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnClass;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnProperty;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

/**
 * 鏂囦欢瀛樺偍鍋ュ悍妫€鏌?
 *
 * <p>妫€娴嬪綋鍓嶆縺娲荤殑 FileStorage 鏄惁鍙敤锛屾毚闇?/actuator/health/file 绔偣銆?
 *
 * <p><b>妫€娴嬮€昏緫锛?/b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>鑾峰彇褰撳墠婵€娲荤殑瀛樺偍宸ュ巶</li>
 *   <li>鎵ц bucket 瀛樺湪鎬ф娴嬮獙璇佽繛鎺?/li>
 *   <li>杩斿洖瀛樺偍绫诲瀷涓庡搷搴旇€楁椂</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 */
@Slf4j
@Configuration
@ConditionalOnClass(IStorageFactory.class)
@ConditionalOnProperty(prefix = "remi.file", name = "enabled", havingValue = "true", matchIfMissing = false)
public class FileStorageHealthIndicator implements HealthIndicator {

    /** 鏂囦欢瀛樺偍閰嶇疆 */
    @Resource
    private FileProperties fileProperties;

    /** 瀛樺偍宸ュ巶瀹炰緥 */
    @Resource
    private IStorageFactory storageFactory;

    /**
     * 鎵ц鏂囦欢瀛樺偍鍋ュ悍妫€鏌?
     * <p>妫€娴嬪綋鍓嶆縺娲荤殑瀛樺偍妗舵槸鍚﹀彲杈撅紝杩斿洖瀛樺偍绫诲瀷銆佹《鍚嶃€佺鐐瑰拰鍝嶅簲鑰楁椂绛夎鎯呫€?
     *
     * @return 鍋ュ悍妫€鏌ョ粨鏋?
     */
    @Override
    public Health health() {
        try {
            long startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();

            IFileStorage storage = storageFactory.getStorage();
            boolean reachable = storage.bucketExists(fileProperties.getBucket());
            long responseTime = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime;

            if (reachable) {
                return Health.up()
                        .withDetail("storageType", fileProperties.getType())
                        .withDetail("bucket", fileProperties.getBucket())
                        .withDetail("endpoint", fileProperties.getEndpoint())
                        .withDetail("responseTimeMs", responseTime)
                        .build();
            }

            return Health.down()
                    .withDetail("storageType", fileProperties.getType())
                    .withDetail("bucket", fileProperties.getBucket())
                    .withDetail("reason", "bucket not accessible")
                    .build();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("鏂囦欢瀛樺偍鍋ュ悍妫€鏌ュけ璐?, e);
            return Health.down()
                    .withDetail("storageType", fileProperties.getType())
                    .withDetail("bucket", fileProperties.getBucket())
                    .withDetail("error", e.getMessage())
                    .build();
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\file\lifecycle\FileLifecycleManager.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.file.lifecycle;

import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.IFileStorage;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.IStorageFactory;
import lombok.Data;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnProperty;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 鏂囦欢鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡绠＄悊鍣?
 *
 * <p>鏀寔鍩轰簬瑙勫垯鐨勮嚜鍔ㄨ繃鏈熸竻鐞嗭紝鎸夋枃浠惰矾寰勫墠缂€閰嶇疆涓嶅悓鐨勪繚鐣欑瓥鐣ワ紝
 * 瀹氭椂鎵弿骞舵竻鐞嗚繃鏈熸枃浠躲€?
 *
 * <p><b>閰嶇疆绀轰緥锛坅pplication.yml锛夛細</b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * remi:
 *   file:
 *     lifecycle:
 *       enabled: true
 *       cron: "0 0 2 * * ?"  # 姣忓ぉ鍑屾櫒2鐐规墽琛?
 *       rules:
 *         - prefix: "temp/"
 *           maxAgeDays: 7
 *           action: delete
 *         - prefix: "logs/"
 *           maxAgeDays: 30
 *           action: delete
 *         - prefix: "archive/"
 *           maxAgeDays: 365
 *           action: delete
 *       dry-run: false
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 */
@Slf4j
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@ConditionalOnProperty(prefix = "remi.file.lifecycle", name = "enabled", havingValue = "true")
public class FileLifecycleManager {

	private final FileLifecycleProperties lifecycleProperties;
	private final IStorageFactory storageFactory;
	private final IFileStorage fileStorage;

	/**
	 * 瀹氭椂鎵ц鏂囦欢娓呯悊浠诲姟
	 *
	 * <p>鏍规嵁閰嶇疆鐨?cron 琛ㄨ揪寮忓畾鏃惰Е鍙戯紝閬嶅巻鎵€鏈夎鍒欏苟鎵ц杩囨湡鏂囦欢娓呯悊銆?
	 */
	@Scheduled(cron = "${remi.file.lifecycle.cron:0 0 2 * * ?}")
	public void executeCleanup() {
		if (!lifecycleProperties.isEnabled()) {
			log.debug("鏂囦欢鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡娓呯悊鏈惎鐢紝璺宠繃鎵ц");
			return;
		}

		List<FileLifecycleProperties.LifecycleRule> rules = lifecycleProperties.getRules();
		if (rules == null || rules.isEmpty()) {
			log.debug("鏂囦欢鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡娓呯悊瑙勫垯涓虹┖锛岃烦杩囨墽琛?);
			return;
		}

		IFileStorage storage = resolveStorage();
		if (storage == null) {
			log.error("鏂囦欢鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡娓呯悊澶辫触锛氭棤娉曡幏鍙栨枃浠跺瓨鍌ㄥ疄渚?);
			return;
		}

		String bucketName = lifecycleProperties.getBucket();
		boolean dryRun = lifecycleProperties.isDryRun();

		log.info("寮€濮嬫墽琛屾枃浠剁敓鍛藉懆鏈熸竻鐞? ruleCount={}, dryRun={}", rules.size(), dryRun);

		for (FileLifecycleProperties.LifecycleRule rule : rules) {
			try {
				processRule(storage, bucketName, rule, dryRun);
			} catch (Exception e) {
				log.error("鏂囦欢鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡娓呯悊瑙勫垯鎵ц澶辫触, prefix={}: {}",
						rule.getPrefix(), e.getMessage(), e);
			}
		}

		log.info("鏂囦欢鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡娓呯悊鎵ц瀹屾垚");
	}

	/**
	 * 鎵嬪姩瑙﹀彂娓呯悊
	 *
	 * @return 娓呯悊缁撴灉缁熻
	 */
	public CleanupResult executeManualCleanup() {
		if (!lifecycleProperties.isEnabled()) {
			return CleanupResult.skipped("鏂囦欢鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡娓呯悊鏈惎鐢?);
		}

		List<FileLifecycleProperties.LifecycleRule> rules = lifecycleProperties.getRules();
		if (rules == null || rules.isEmpty()) {
			return CleanupResult.skipped("鏂囦欢鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡娓呯悊瑙勫垯涓虹┖");
		}

		IFileStorage storage = resolveStorage();
		if (storage == null) {
			return CleanupResult.failed("鏃犳硶鑾峰彇鏂囦欢瀛樺偍瀹炰緥");
		}

		String bucketName = lifecycleProperties.getBucket();
		boolean dryRun = lifecycleProperties.isDryRun();
		CleanupResult result = new CleanupResult();

		for (FileLifecycleProperties.LifecycleRule rule : rules) {
			try {
				processRuleWithStats(storage, bucketName, rule, dryRun, result);
			} catch (Exception e) {
				log.error("鏂囦欢鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡娓呯悊瑙勫垯鎵ц澶辫触, prefix={}: {}",
						rule.getPrefix(), e.getMessage(), e);
				result.addError(rule.getPrefix(), e.getMessage());
			}
		}

		return result;
	}

	/**
	 * 澶勭悊鍗曟潯鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡瑙勫垯
	 *
	 * @param storage    鏂囦欢瀛樺偍瀹炰緥
	 * @param bucketName 瀛樺偍妗跺悕绉?
	 * @param rule       鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡瑙勫垯
	 * @param dryRun     鏄惁浠呮ā鎷熸墽琛?
	 */
	private void processRule(IFileStorage storage, String bucketName,
			FileLifecycleProperties.LifecycleRule rule, boolean dryRun) {
		String prefix = rule.getPrefix();
		long maxAgeMillis = rule.getMaxAgeDays() * 24L * 60L * 60L * 1000L;
		long cutoffTime = System.currentTimeMillis() - maxAgeMillis;

		log.info("澶勭悊鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡瑙勫垯: prefix={}, maxAgeDays={}", prefix, rule.getMaxAgeDays());

		// 鍒楀嚭鎸囧畾鍓嶇紑涓嬬殑鎵€鏈夊璞?
		String cursor = null;
		int scannedCount = 0;
		int deletedCount = 0;
		int skippedCount = 0;

		while (true) {
			com.remisoft.comm.file.domain.ListObjectsResult listResult =
					storage.listObjects(bucketName, prefix, cursor, 1000);

			if (listResult == null || listResult.getObjects() == null || listResult.getObjects().isEmpty()) {
				break;
			}

			for (com.remisoft.comm.file.domain.ObjectMetadata obj : listResult.getObjects()) {
				scannedCount++;
				long lastModified = obj.getLastModified() != null
						? obj.getLastModified().atZone(ZoneId.systemDefault()).toInstant().toEpochMilli()
						: 0;

				if (lastModified > 0 && lastModified < cutoffTime) {
					if (dryRun) {
						log.info("[DryRun] 璺宠繃鍒犻櫎杩囨湡鏂囦欢: objectName={}, lastModified={}",
								obj.getObjectName(), obj.getLastModified());
						skippedCount++;
					} else {
						try {
							storage.delete(bucketName, obj.getObjectName());
							deletedCount++;
							log.debug("鍒犻櫎杩囨湡鏂囦欢: objectName={}", obj.getObjectName());
						} catch (Exception e) {
							log.error("鍒犻櫎杩囨湡鏂囦欢澶辫触: objectName={}, error={}", obj.getObjectName(), e.getMessage());
						}
					}
				} else {
					skippedCount++;
				}
			}

			cursor = listResult.getNextCursor();
			if (cursor == null || cursor.isEmpty()) {
				break;
			}
		}

		log.info("鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡瑙勫垯鎵ц瀹屾垚: prefix={}, scanned={}, deleted={}, skipped={}",
				prefix, scannedCount, deletedCount, skippedCount);
	}

	/**
	 * 澶勭悊鍗曟潯瑙勫垯骞剁粺璁＄粨鏋?
	 */
	private void processRuleWithStats(IFileStorage storage, String bucketName,
			FileLifecycleProperties.LifecycleRule rule, boolean dryRun, CleanupResult result) {
		String prefix = rule.getPrefix();
		long maxAgeMillis = rule.getMaxAgeDays() * 24L * 60L * 60L * 1000L;
		long cutoffTime = System.currentTimeMillis() - maxAgeMillis;

		String cursor = null;

		while (true) {
			com.remisoft.comm.file.domain.ListObjectsResult listResult =
					storage.listObjects(bucketName, prefix, cursor, 1000);

			if (listResult == null || listResult.getObjects() == null || listResult.getObjects().isEmpty()) {
				break;
			}

			for (com.remisoft.comm.file.domain.ObjectMetadata obj : listResult.getObjects()) {
				long lastModified = obj.getLastModified() != null
						? obj.getLastModified().atZone(ZoneId.systemDefault()).toInstant().toEpochMilli()
						: 0;

				if (lastModified > 0 && lastModified < cutoffTime) {
					if (dryRun) {
						result.incrementSkipped();
					} else {
						try {
							storage.delete(bucketName, obj.getObjectName());
							result.incrementDeleted();
						} catch (Exception e) {
							result.addError(obj.getObjectName(), e.getMessage());
						}
					}
				}
				result.incrementScanned();
			}

			cursor = listResult.getNextCursor();
			if (cursor == null || cursor.isEmpty()) {
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	/**
	 * 瑙ｆ瀽鏂囦欢瀛樺偍瀹炰緥
	 *
	 * @return 鏂囦欢瀛樺偍瀹炰緥
	 */
	private IFileStorage resolveStorage() {
		if (fileStorage != null) {
			return fileStorage;
		}
		if (storageFactory != null) {
			return storageFactory.getStorage();
		}
		return null;
	}

	/**
	 * 娓呯悊缁撴灉缁熻
	 */
	@Data
	public static class CleanupResult {

		private int scannedCount = 0;
		private int deletedCount = 0;
		private int skippedCount = 0;
		private boolean success = true;
		private String message;
		private List<String> errors = new ArrayList<>();

		public CleanupResult() {
		}

		public static CleanupResult skipped(String message) {
			CleanupResult result = new CleanupResult();
			result.success = true;
			result.message = message;
			return result;
		}

		public static CleanupResult failed(String message) {
			CleanupResult result = new CleanupResult();
			result.success = false;
			result.message = message;
			return result;
		}

		public void incrementScanned() {
			scannedCount++;
		}

		public void incrementDeleted() {
			deletedCount++;
		}

		public void incrementSkipped() {
			skippedCount++;
		}

		public void addError(String path, String error) {
			errors.add(path + ": " + error);
		}
	}

	/**
	 * 鏂囦欢鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡瑙勫垯閰嶇疆
	 *
	 * <p>閰嶇疆鍓嶇紑: {@code remi.file.lifecycle}
	 */
	@Data
	@Component
	@ConfigurationProperties(prefix = "remi.file.lifecycle")
	public static class FileLifecycleProperties {

		/**
		 * 鏄惁鍚敤鏂囦欢鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡绠＄悊
		 */
		private boolean enabled = false;

		/**
		 * 瀹氭椂浠诲姟 cron 琛ㄨ揪寮?
		 */
		private String cron = "0 0 2 * * ?";

		/**
		 * 瀛樺偍妗跺悕绉?
		 */
		private String bucket;

		/**
		 * 鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡瑙勫垯鍒楄〃
		 */
		private List<LifecycleRule> rules = new ArrayList<>();

		/**
		 * 鏄惁浠呮ā鎷熸墽琛岋紙涓嶅疄闄呭垹闄わ級
		 */
		private boolean dryRun = false;

		/**
		 * 鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡瑙勫垯
		 */
		@Data
		public static class LifecycleRule {

			/**
			 * 鏂囦欢璺緞鍓嶇紑鍖归厤锛堝 "temp/", "logs/"锛?
			 */
			private String prefix;

			/**
			 * 鏈€澶т繚鐣欏ぉ鏁帮紝瓒呰繃姝ゅぉ鏁扮殑鏂囦欢灏嗚娓呯悊
			 */
			private int maxAgeDays;

			/**
			 * 娓呯悊鍔ㄤ綔锛屽綋鍓嶄粎鏀寔 delete
			 */
			private String action = "delete";
		}
	}
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\file\service\DedupCleanupScheduler.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.file.service;

import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;

/**
 * 鍘婚噸璁板綍娓呯悊璋冨害鍣?
 *
 * <p>瀹氭椂娓呯悊杩囨湡鐨勬枃浠跺幓閲嶏紙绉掍紶锛塎D5 鏄犲皠璁板綍銆?
 * 鐢?{@link com.remisoft.comm.file.config.FileConfiguration} 閫氳繃 @Bean 娉ㄥ唽锛?
 * 浠呭綋 {@link FileDedupService} 瀛樺湪鏃剁敓鏁堛€?
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 */
public class DedupCleanupScheduler {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(DedupCleanupScheduler.class);

    private final FileDedupService fileDedupService;

    public DedupCleanupScheduler(FileDedupService fileDedupService) {
        this.fileDedupService = fileDedupService;
    }

    /**
     * 瀹氭椂娓呯悊杩囨湡鐨勫幓閲嶆槧灏勮褰曪紙姣忓ぉ鍑屾櫒3鐐规墽琛岋級
     */
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 0 3 * * ?")
    public void cleanupExpiredDedupEntries() {
        log.debug("寮€濮嬫竻鐞嗚繃鏈熺殑鏂囦欢鍘婚噸鏄犲皠璁板綍");
        try {
            fileDedupService.cleanupExpiredEntries();
            log.debug("鏂囦欢鍘婚噸鏄犲皠璁板綍娓呯悊瀹屾垚");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("鏂囦欢鍘婚噸鏄犲皠璁板綍娓呯悊澶辫触: {}", e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\file\service\FileDedupService.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.file.service;

import com.remisoft.comm.exception.custom.SysException;
import com.remisoft.comm.file.storage.IFileStorage;
import com.remisoft.comm.redis.service.ops.RedisStringOps;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnBean;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.io.InputStream;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.security.NoSuchAlgorithmException;
import java.time.Duration;

/**
 * 鏂囦欢鍘婚噸鏈嶅姟锛堢浼狅級
 *
 * 鍩轰簬鏂囦欢 SHA-256 鍜屾枃浠跺ぇ灏忓疄鐜扮浼犲姛鑳姐€備笂浼犲墠鍏堣绠?SHA-256锛? * 濡傛灉鐩稿悓澶у皬涓旂浉鍚?SHA-256 鐨勬枃浠跺凡瀛樺湪锛岀洿鎺ヨ繑鍥炲凡鏈夋枃浠朵俊鎭€? * 閲囩敤鏂囦欢澶у皬 + SHA-256 鍙岄噸鏍￠獙锛岄伩鍏嶅崟涓€鍝堝笇纰版挒瀵艰嚧鐨勫唴瀹规浛鎹㈤闄┿€? *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 */
@Service
@ConditionalOnBean({RedisStringOps.class, IFileStorage.class})
public class FileDedupService {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(FileDedupService.class);
    private static final String DEDUP_KEY_PREFIX = "file:dedup:hash:";

    private final RedisStringOps redisStringOps;
    @SuppressWarnings("unused")
    private final IFileStorage fileStorage;

    public FileDedupService(RedisStringOps redisStringOps, IFileStorage fileStorage) {
        this.redisStringOps = redisStringOps;
        this.fileStorage = fileStorage;
    }

    /**
     * 璁＄畻杈撳叆娴佺殑 SHA-256 鎽樿
     */
    public String calculateHash(InputStream inputStream) throws NoSuchAlgorithmException {
        MessageDigest digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
        byte[] buffer = new byte[8192];
        int len;
        try {
            while ((len = inputStream.read(buffer)) != -1) {
                digest.update(buffer, 0, len);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new SysException("Failed to calculate SHA-256", e);
        }
        byte[] digestBytes = digest.digest();
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (byte b : digestBytes) {
            sb.append(String.format("%02x", b));
        }
        return sb.toString();
    }

    /**
     * 鏋勫缓鍘婚噸 Key锛堟枃浠跺ぇ灏?SHA-256 鍙岄噸鏍￠獙锛?     *
     * @param fileSize 鏂囦欢澶у皬锛堝瓧鑺傦級
     * @param hash     鏂囦欢 SHA-256 鎽樿
     * @return 鍘婚噸 Key
     */
    private String buildDedupKey(long fileSize, String hash) {
        return DEDUP_KEY_PREFIX + fileSize + ":" + hash;
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ユ枃浠舵槸鍚﹀凡瀛樺湪锛堢浼狅級
     *
     * @param fileSize 鏂囦欢澶у皬锛堝瓧鑺傦級
     * @param hash     鏂囦欢 SHA-256 鎽樿
     * @return 宸插瓨鍦ㄧ殑鏂囦欢璺緞锛屽鏋滀笉瀛樺湪杩斿洖 null
     */
    public String checkExisting(long fileSize, String hash) {
        String key = buildDedupKey(fileSize, hash);
        String existingPath = redisStringOps.get(key, String.class);
        return existingPath;
    }

    /**
     * 娉ㄥ唽鏂囦欢鍝堝笇鏄犲皠
     *
     * @param fileSize 鏂囦欢澶у皬锛堝瓧鑺傦級
     * @param hash     鏂囦欢 SHA-256 鎽樿
     * @param filePath 鏂囦欢瀛樺偍璺緞
     */
    public void registerHash(long fileSize, String hash, String filePath) {
        String key = buildDedupKey(fileSize, hash);
        // 璁剧疆 30 澶╄繃鏈?        redisStringOps.set(key, filePath, Duration.ofDays(30));
    }

    /**
     * 娓呯悊杩囨湡鐨勫幓閲嶆槧灏勮褰?     *
     * <p>鐢变簬鍘婚噸鏄犲皠鍦?Redis 涓凡璁剧疆 TTL锛屾鏂规硶涓昏鐢ㄤ簬
     * 璁板綍娓呯悊鏃ュ織鍜屾墽琛岄澶栫殑娓呯悊閫昏緫銆?     */
    public void cleanupExpiredEntries() {
        log.debug("鍘婚噸鏄犲皠璁板綍鍩轰簬 Redis TTL 鑷姩杩囨湡锛屾棤闇€鎵嬪姩娓呯悊");
    }
}

package com.remisoft.comm.file.storage;

import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

/**
 * 鍒嗙墖涓婁紶妯℃澘鎶借薄绫?
 *
 * <p>鎻愬彇鍒嗙墖涓婁紶鍒濆鍖栥€佹柇鐐圭画浼犮€佽繘搴︾鐞嗙殑閫氱敤閫昏緫锛?
 * 鍏蜂綋瀛樺偍瀹炵幇鍙渶瀹炵幇鏂偣妫€鏌ョ偣淇濆瓨/鍔犺浇鐨勬娊璞℃柟娉曘€?
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 */
public abstract class AbstractChunkedUploadTemplate {

    protected final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(getClass());

    private final long checkpointTtlMillis;

    protected AbstractChunkedUploadTemplate(long checkpointTtlMillis) {
        this.checkpointTtlMillis = checkpointTtlMillis;
    }

    /**
     * 鍒濆鍖栧垎鐗囦笂浼犲苟鐢熸垚鏂偣妫€鏌ョ偣
     *
     * @param bucketName   瀛樺偍妗跺悕绉?
     * @param objectName   瀵硅薄鍚嶇О
     * @param uploadId     鍒嗙墖涓婁紶 ID
     * @param partSize     鍒嗙墖澶у皬锛堝瓧鑺傦級
     * @param totalSize    鎬绘枃浠跺ぇ灏忥紙瀛楄妭锛?
     * @return 鍒嗙墖涓婁紶鍒濆鍖栫粨鏋?
     */
    public ChunkedUploadResult initChunkedUpload(String bucketName, String objectName,
                                                  String uploadId, long partSize, long totalSize) {
        if (totalSize <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("鏂囦欢澶у皬蹇呴』澶т簬 0");
        }
        if (partSize <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("鍒嗙墖澶у皬蹇呴』澶т簬 0");
        }

        int totalParts = (int) Math.ceil((double) totalSize / partSize);
        long expiresAt = System.currentTimeMillis() + checkpointTtlMillis;

        saveCheckpoint(bucketName, objectName, uploadId, partSize, totalSize, totalParts, 0, expiresAt);

        log.info("[ChunkedUpload] init, bucket={}, object={}, uploadId={}, totalSize={}, partSize={}, totalParts={}",
                bucketName, objectName, uploadId, totalSize, partSize, totalParts);

        return ChunkedUploadResult.init(uploadId, totalParts, partSize, totalSize);
    }

    /**
     * 鍔犺浇鏂偣缁紶妫€鏌ョ偣
     *
     * @param bucketName 瀛樺偍妗跺悕绉?
     * @param objectName 瀵硅薄鍚嶇О
     * @param uploadId   鍒嗙墖涓婁紶 ID
     * @return 妫€鏌ョ偣淇℃伅锛屼笉瀛樺湪杩斿洖 null
     */
    public ChunkedUploadCheckpoint loadCheckpoint(String bucketName, String objectName, String uploadId) {
        return doLoadCheckpoint(bucketName, objectName, uploadId);
    }

    /**
     * 淇濆瓨鏂偣缁紶妫€鏌ョ偣锛堝瓙绫诲疄鐜帮級
     *
     * @param bucketName    瀛樺偍妗跺悕绉?
     * @param objectName    瀵硅薄鍚嶇О
     * @param uploadId      鍒嗙墖涓婁紶 ID
     * @param partSize      鍒嗙墖澶у皬
     * @param totalSize     鎬绘枃浠跺ぇ灏?
     * @param totalParts    鎬诲垎鐗囨暟
     * @param completedParts 宸插畬鎴愮殑鍒嗙墖鏁?
     * @param expiresAt     杩囨湡鏃堕棿鎴?
     */
    protected abstract void saveCheckpoint(String bucketName, String objectName, String uploadId,
                                            long partSize, long totalSize, int totalParts,
                                            int completedParts, long expiresAt);

    /**
     * 鍔犺浇鏂偣缁紶妫€鏌ョ偣锛堝瓙绫诲疄鐜帮級
     */
    protected abstract ChunkedUploadCheckpoint doLoadCheckpoint(String bucketName, String objectName, String uploadId);

    /**
     * 鍒犻櫎鏂偣缁紶妫€鏌ョ偣锛堝瓙绫诲疄鐜帮級
     */
    protected abstract void deleteCheckpoint(String bucketName, String objectName, String uploadId);

    /**
     * 鍒嗙墖涓婁紶鍒濆鍖栫粨鏋?
     */
    public static class ChunkedUploadResult {
        private final String uploadId;
        private final int totalParts;
        private final long partSize;
        private final long totalSize;

        private ChunkedUploadResult(String uploadId, int totalParts, long partSize, long totalSize) {
            this.uploadId = uploadId;
            this.totalParts = totalParts;
            this.partSize = partSize;
            this.totalSize = totalSize;
        }

        public static ChunkedUploadResult init(String uploadId, int totalParts, long partSize, long totalSize) {
            return new ChunkedUploadResult(uploadId, totalParts, partSize, totalSize);
        }

        public String getUploadId() { return uploadId; }
        public int getTotalParts() { return totalParts; }
        public long getPartSize() { return partSize; }
        public long getTotalSize() { return totalSize; }
    }

    /**
     * 鏂偣缁紶妫€鏌ョ偣淇℃伅
     */
    public static class ChunkedUploadCheckpoint {
        private final String uploadId;
        private final long partSize;
        private final long totalSize;
        private final int totalParts;
        private final int completedParts;
        private final long expiresAt;

        public ChunkedUploadCheckpoint(String uploadId, long partSize, long totalSize,
                                        int totalParts, int completedParts, long expiresAt) {
            this.uploadId = uploadId;
            this.partSize = partSize;
            this.totalSize = totalSize;
            this.totalParts = totalParts;
            this.completedParts = completedParts;
            this.expiresAt = expiresAt;
        }

        public String getUploadId() { return uploadId; }
        public long getPartSize() { return partSize; }
        public long getTotalSize() { return totalSize; }
        public int getTotalParts() { return totalParts; }
        public int getCompletedParts() { return completedParts; }
        public long getExpiresAt() { return expiresAt; }

        public boolean isExpired() {
            return System.currentTimeMillis() > expiresAt;
        }
    }
}

    }

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓榛樿鐨勫垎鐗囦笂浼犳ā鏉垮疄鐜?
     * <p>鍩轰簬 checkpointStore 淇濆瓨/鍔犺浇妫€鏌ョ偣鏁版嵁
     */
    protected final AbstractChunkedUploadTemplate createChunkedUploadTemplate() {
        final AbstractFileStorage self = this;
        return new AbstractChunkedUploadTemplate(CHECKPOINT_TTL_SECONDS * 1000L) {
            @Override
            protected void saveCheckpoint(String bucketName, String objectName, String uploadId,
                                          long partSize, long totalSize, int totalParts,
                                          int completedParts, long expiresAt) {
                // 濮旀墭缁?AbstractFileStorage 鐨勬鏌ョ偣淇濆瓨閫昏緫
                UploadCheckpoint checkpoint = new UploadCheckpoint();
                checkpoint.setUploadId(uploadId);
                checkpoint.setBucketName(bucketName);
                checkpoint.setObjectName(objectName);
                checkpoint.setPartSize(partSize);
                checkpoint.setTotalSize(totalSize);
                checkpoint.setUploadedPartsCount(completedParts);
                self.saveCheckpoint(checkpoint);
            }

            @Override
            protected AbstractChunkedUploadTemplate.ChunkedUploadCheckpoint doLoadCheckpoint(
                    String bucketName, String objectName, String uploadId) {
                UploadCheckpoint loaded = self.loadCheckpoint(bucketName, objectName);
                if (loaded == null) {
                    return null;
                }
                long partSize = loaded.getPartSize() != null ? loaded.getPartSize() : 0;
                long totalSize = loaded.getTotalSize() != null ? loaded.getTotalSize() : 0;
                int totalParts = partSize > 0 && totalSize > 0 ? (int) Math.ceil((double) totalSize / partSize) : 0;
                return new AbstractChunkedUploadTemplate.ChunkedUploadCheckpoint(
                        uploadId,
                        partSize,
                        totalSize,
                        totalParts,
                        loaded.getUploadedPartsCount() != null ? loaded.getUploadedPartsCount() : 0,
                        System.currentTimeMillis() + CHECKPOINT_TTL_SECONDS * 1000L);
            }

            @Override
            protected void deleteCheckpoint(String bucketName, String objectName, String uploadId) {
                UploadCheckpoint checkpoint = new UploadCheckpoint();
                checkpoint.setBucketName(bucketName);
                checkpoint.setObjectName(objectName);
                self.deleteCheckpoint(checkpoint);
            }
        };
    }

    /**
     * 璁剧疆骞跺彂涓婁紶淇濇姢鍣?
     */
    public void setConcurrencyGuard(UploadConcurrencyGuard guard) {
        this.concurrencyGuard = guard;
    }

    /**
     * 娓呯悊杩囨湡鐨勫垎鐗囦笂浼犱笂涓嬫枃
     * <p>寤鸿瀹氭椂璋冪敤锛堝姣忓皬鏃朵竴娆★級娓呯悊瓒呮椂鏈畬鎴愮殑涓婁紶浠诲姟
     *
     * @param timeoutMinutes 瓒呮椂鏃堕棿锛堝垎閽燂級锛岃秴杩囨鏃堕棿鏈洿鏂扮殑涓婁笅鏂囧皢琚竻鐞?
     */
    public void cleanExpiredMultipartContexts(int timeoutMinutes) {
        MultipartContextStore store = multipartContextStore;
        if (store != null) {
            store.cleanExpired(timeoutMinutes);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean bucketExists(String bucketName) {
        String resolvedBucket = resolveBucketName(bucketName);
        if (StringUtils.isBlank(resolvedBucket)) {
            return false;
        }
        return doBucketExists(resolvedBucket);
    }

    @Override
    public void makeBucket(String bucketName) {
        String resolvedBucket = resolveBucketName(bucketName);
        if (StringUtils.isBlank(resolvedBucket)) {
            throw new BusinessException(FileExceptionCode.BUCKET_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        if (!bucketExists(resolvedBucket)) {
            doMakeBucket(resolvedBucket);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean folderExists(String bucketName, String objectName) {
        String resolvedBucket = resolveBucketName(bucketName);
        String resolvedObjectName = resolveObjectKey(resolvedBucket, objectName);
        return doFolderExists(resolvedBucket, resolvedObjectName);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean objectExists(String bucketName, String objectName) {
        String resolvedBucket = resolveBucketName(bucketName);
        String resolvedObjectName = resolveObjectKey(resolvedBucket, objectName);
        try {
            ObjectMetadata metadata = doGetMetadata(resolvedBucket, resolvedObjectName);
            return metadata != null;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public ObjectMetadata getMetadata(String bucketName, String objectName) {
        String resolvedBucket = resolveBucketName(bucketName);
        String resolvedObjectName = resolveObjectKey(resolvedBucket, objectName);
        return doGetMetadata(resolvedBucket, resolvedObjectName);
    }

    @Override
    public void copyObject(String srcBucketName, String srcObjectName, String destBucketName, String destObjectName) {
        String resolvedSrcBucket = resolveBucketName(srcBucketName);
        String resolvedSrcObject = resolveObjectKey(resolvedSrcBucket, srcObjectName);
        String resolvedDestBucket = resolveBucketName(destBucketName);
        String resolvedDestObject = resolveObjectKey(resolvedDestBucket, destObjectName);

        try {
            ObjectMetadata metadata = doGetMetadata(resolvedSrcBucket, resolvedSrcObject);
            if (metadata == null) {
                throw new BusinessException(FileExceptionCode.FILE_NOT_FOUND);
            }
            String contentType = metadata.getContentType() != null ? metadata.getContentType() : "application/octet-stream";
            long size = metadata.getSize();
            try (InputStream is = doGetObject(resolvedSrcBucket, resolvedSrcObject, null, null)) {
                doPutObject(resolvedDestBucket, resolvedDestObject, is, size, contentType);
            }
            log.info("[Storage] copyObject success, src={}/{}, dest={}/{}",
                    resolvedSrcBucket, resolvedSrcObject, resolvedDestBucket, resolvedDestObject);
        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            throw e;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[Storage] copyObject failed, src={}/{}, dest={}/{}, message={}",
                    resolvedSrcBucket, resolvedSrcObject, resolvedDestBucket, resolvedDestObject, e.getMessage());
            throw new BusinessException(FileExceptionCode.OBJECT_COPY_FAILED);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void moveObject(String srcBucketName, String srcObjectName, String destBucketName, String destObjectName) {
        copyObject(srcBucketName, srcObjectName, destBucketName, destObjectName);
        delete(srcBucketName, srcObjectName);
        log.info("[Storage] moveObject success, src={}/{}, dest={}/{}",
                resolveBucketName(srcBucketName), resolveObjectKey(resolveBucketName(srcBucketName), srcObjectName),
                resolveBucketName(destBucketName), resolveObjectKey(resolveBucketName(destBucketName), destObjectName));
    }

    @Override
    public ListObjectsResult listObjects(String bucketName, String prefix, String cursor, int maxKeys) {
        String resolvedBucket = resolveBucketName(bucketName);
        return doListObjects(resolvedBucket, prefix, cursor, maxKeys);
    }

    @Override
    public void makeFolder(String bucketName, String objectName) {
        String resolvedBucket = resolveBucketName(bucketName);
        String resolvedObjectName = resolveObjectKey(resolvedBucket, objectName);
        if (!folderExists(resolvedBucket, resolvedObjectName)) {
            doMakeFolder(resolvedBucket, resolvedObjectName);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public FileStorage upload(String bucketName, String objectName, MultipartFile file) {
        return upload(bucketName, objectName, file, null);
    }

    @Override
    public FileStorage upload(String bucketName, String objectName, MultipartFile file, UploadProgressListener listener) {
        String resolvedBucket = resolveBucketName(bucketName);
        String resolvedObjectName = resolveObjectKey(resolvedBucket, objectName);

        if (file.isEmpty()) {
            throw new BusinessException(FileExceptionCode.FILE_EMPTY);
        }

        FileTypeValidator.validate(file);

        // 鑾峰彇骞跺彂涓婁紶閿?
        String lockToken = acquireConcurrencyLock(resolvedObjectName);

        makeBucket(resolvedBucket);
        FileStorage fileStorage = buildFileStorage(file);
        long totalBytes = file.getSize();

        if (listener != null) {
            listener.onStart(totalBytes);
        }

        try (InputStream inputStream = file.getInputStream()) {
            doPutObject(resolvedBucket, resolvedObjectName, inputStream,
                    file.getSize(), file.getContentType());

            fileStorage.setUuidName(resolvedObjectName);
            fileStorage.setUrl(buildObjectUrl(resolvedBucket, resolvedObjectName));

            if (listener != null) {
                listener.onSuccess(resolvedObjectName);
            }
            return fileStorage;
        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            if (listener != null) {
                listener.onFailure(resolvedObjectName, e);
            }
            throw e;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[Storage] file upload failed, bucket={}, object={}, message={}",
                    resolvedBucket, resolvedObjectName, e.getMessage(), e);
            if (listener != null) {
                listener.onFailure(resolvedObjectName, e);
            }
            throw new BusinessException(FileExceptionCode.FILE_UPLOAD_FAILED);
        } finally {
            releaseConcurrencyLock(resolvedObjectName, lockToken);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void delete(String bucketName, String objectName) {
        String resolvedBucket = resolveBucketName(bucketName);
        String resolvedObjectName = resolveObjectKey(resolvedBucket, objectName);

        if (StringUtils.isEmpty(resolvedObjectName)) {
            throw new BusinessException(FileExceptionCode.FILE_PATH_EMPTY);
        }

        try {
            doRemoveObject(resolvedBucket, resolvedObjectName);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[Storage] file delete failed, bucket={}, object={}, message={}",
                    resolvedBucket, resolvedObjectName, e.getMessage(), e);
            throw new BusinessException(FileExceptionCode.FILE_DELETE_FAILED);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public BatchDeleteResult batchDelete(String bucketName, List<String> objectNames) {
        if (objectNames == null || objectNames.isEmpty()) {
            return BatchDeleteResult.allSuccess(Collections.emptyList());
        }
        List<String> successList = new ArrayList<>();
        Map<String, String> failedMap = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        for (String objectName : objectNames) {
            try {
                delete(bucketName, objectName);
                successList.add(objectName);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                String errorMsg = e.getMessage() != null ? e.getMessage() : e.getClass().getSimpleName();
                failedMap.put(objectName, errorMsg);
                log.error("[Storage] batch delete failed, object={}, message={}", objectName, errorMsg, e);
            }
        }
        return new BatchDeleteResult(List.copyOf(successList), Map.copyOf(failedMap));
    }

    @Override
    public void download(String bucketName, String objectName, HttpServletResponse response) {
        download(bucketName, objectName, response, null, null);
    }

    @Override
    public void download(String bucketName, String objectName, HttpServletResponse response, Long offset, Long length) {
        String resolvedBucket = resolveBucketName(bucketName);
        String resolvedObjectName = resolveObjectKey(resolvedBucket, objectName);

        try (InputStream is = doGetObject(resolvedBucket, resolvedObjectName, offset, length);
             OutputStream os = response.getOutputStream()) {
            byte[] buffer = new byte[8192];
            int read;
            while ((read = is.read(buffer)) != -1) {
                os.write(buffer, 0, read);
            }
            os.flush();
            log.info("[Storage] file download success, bucket={}, object={}", resolvedBucket, resolvedObjectName);
        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            throw e;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[Storage] file download failed, bucket={}, object={}, message={}",
                    resolvedBucket, resolvedObjectName, e.getMessage(), e);
            throw new BusinessException(FileExceptionCode.FILE_DOWNLOAD_FAILED);
        }
    }

            return doGetObject(resolvedBucket, resolvedObjectName, null, null);
        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            throw e;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[Storage] downloadAsStream failed, bucket={}, object={}, message={}",
